### 2026-07-15 — Unified logging: one format, one file, level in Settings
- **Context:** logging was scattered across 6 destinations with no level control and no uniform structure (desktop `debug.log` in `[unix_ms]` format, `tracing` to stdout, 123 raw frontend `console.*`, Go server stdlib `log` to `insights.log`, CLI `hook-debug.log` in `unix_secs` format, provisioning `mobile-install.log` raw). Yossi asked for: a mandatory logger everywhere, a debug/info setting in Settings, everything readable in ONE place, and a uniform line format that names the writing component.
- **Decisions (Yossi, 2026-07-15):** (1) "one place" = **physical aggregation** — a 60s desktop sync pulls new lines from the 3 remote logs into the local `debug.log` (remote files stay as backup/fallback); (2) Settings exposes only **debug|info** (internally 4 levels; warn/error always written); (3) **full sweep** of all call sites in all layers.
- **Outcome:** unified line format `[YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM:SS.mmm +TZ] [LEVEL] [COMP] msg` everywhere. `winmux_core::log_debug/info/warn/error(tag, msg)` + global `AtomicU8` threshold (`dlog`/`dlog_tag` remain as info-level shims, zero in-tree callers). Frontend `app/src/logger.ts` (`createLogger(tag)`) → `ui_log` command (replaced `diag_log` + `dev_console_log`; ring buffer for `winmux dev console-tail` retained). Go server migrated to `log/slog` with a custom handler (`internal/logging`) + 30s watcher on `~/.winmux/log-level`. CLI `hook_log(level, msg)` reads the same file (`WINMUX_HOOK_VERBOSE` still forces debug). Level propagates: instantly local (settings save funnel), on connect / addon install / level change to remotes (`log_sync::push_log_level`). New `src/log_sync.rs`: per-host byte cursors in `log-sync.json` (atomic), one combined exec per host, 256KB/file/cycle cap, truncate-detection resets near EOF, partial trailing lines held back. Settings → Logs: level select + remote-sync checkbox (i18n ×4). Doctor's recent-errors filter tightened to the `] [ERROR] [` column. This also closes Phase 73's deferred tag sweep.
- **Note:** Go server changes require rebaking `resources/winmux-server-linux-*` (and the CLI `winmux-linux-x64`) via the cross-compile pipeline before they reach users. *(2026-08-23: this is no longer a rule people have to remember — `ci-windows.yml` cross-builds both arches on every push and fails the job if `server/**` moved without the blobs moving too. The rebake itself is still manual; the workflow uploads the binaries as an artifact so it costs a download rather than a local Go toolchain.)*
- **Incident note:** the original implementation worktree (`remote-control-ff33ac`) was deleted from disk with all work uncommitted before the commit step; everything was rebuilt from the session context + re-run sweeps in the `unified-logging` worktree. Lesson: commit per phase in long worktree sessions.

### 2026-07-15 — Phase 80: unified setup wizard + local smart install + WSL-tmux workspaces (closes 6.2)
- **Context:** Yossi reopened the parked 6.2 thread ("two separate buttons — סביבת עבודה חדשה + הקם שרת — is wrong; ONE button with mode selectors"; was waiting on screenshots) and extended it: smart install must also work LOCALLY, including "לוגיקה כמו tmux ללוקאלי" — full feature now.
- **Decisions (Yossi, this session):** ONE "+ סביבת עבודה חדשה" button → mode tree **server (new / existing)** / **local (existing / new)**. Quick-SSH form folds into server→existing as the "I have a key" path (password path = ConnectExistingFlow). Local→existing = plain cmd/PowerShell (old local wizard); local→new = smart install: Claude Code (official `irm claude.ai/install.ps1` — verified, no admin, `%USERPROFILE%\.local\bin`), Git+Node via winget, Codex+Gemini via npm -g, local winmux hooks, and a persistent WSL+tmux environment.
- **Design outcomes:**
  - `SetupWizard.tsx` host composes body-only flows (ProvisionNewServerFlow = stripped ProvisioningWizard, QuickSshCreateFlow, ConnectExistingFlow, QuickLocalCreateFlow, LocalSetupFlow). Null-default mode tree preserves the 65.R root-provisioning guard; Back = unmount = fresh forms. CreateWorkspaceModal is edit-only.
  - **`Connection::Wsl { distro }`** — third variant; WSL panes attach tmux via the SAME 900ms script SSH panes use (shared `build_tmux_attach_script`), transported over wsl.exe. Persistence scope stated honestly in UI copy: survives app restarts, NOT `wsl --shutdown`/Windows reboots. ⚠️ Older builds can't read a workspaces.json containing a wsl workspace — release-note line owed.
  - Local engine (`local_setup.rs`) mirrors provisioning's StepProgress on `local-setup:progress/complete`; Ubuntu OOBE bypassed programmatically (CreateWslUser: uid-1000 as root + `/etc/wsl.conf [user]` + terminate); `wsl --install` relies on its own UAC self-elevation, `ElevationRequired` card as fallback; CLI deploy = stdin-pipe of the embedded winmux-linux-x64 (no `\\wsl$`).
  - Hooks from inside WSL: TCP→named-pipe bridge (per-run HMAC token, lazy start), dial address via `wslinfo --networking-mode` (mirrored/wsl1 → 127.0.0.1, NAT → gateway). CLI patched for native-Windows hooks (HOME→USERPROFILE, quoted `current_exe()` hook command).
- **Outcome / Commits:** `49d127f` (80.A wizard), `f020c54` (80.B Wsl+spawn), `58f5b4c` (80.C engine), `6155f77` (80.D bridge+CLI). tsc + cargo check + 104 lib tests + 27 types tests green; i18n 922 keys ×4.
- **Needs live smoke-test (Yossi):** all 5 wizard leaves; WSL chain on a machine with/without WSL; pane persistence across app restart; local+WSL hooks → permission card; edit modal on ssh/local/wsl workspaces.

### 2026-07-15 — Netfree TLS fix: rustls + `native-certs`, NOT native-tls (revises f00fa1f)
- **Context:** `f00fa1f` (beta.3-netfree) switched ureq to `default-features = false, features = ["native-tls"]` so the updater trusts the Windows Certificate Store (Netfree root). A parallel session hit a build error, and the investigation found the deeper problem too.
- **What was wrong:** (1) build error — `winmux-core/src/http.rs` uses ureq but its Cargo.toml never declared it (E0433); (2) runtime — in ureq 2.x the `native-tls` feature is inert unless the caller builds an Agent with `tls_connector(...)`; with `tls` (rustls) disabled, the default agent has NO TLS backend, so every `ureq::get()` HTTPS call fails. The netfree fix as committed would have broken the updater for *all* users.
- **Options:** (A) wire native-tls properly — manual Agent at every call site, and it reintroduces the SChannel AV/EDR-interception bug that v0.2.3 fixed; **(B) ureq's `native-certs` feature** — rustls stays, but trust roots load from the Windows Certificate Store (`rustls-native-certs`) instead of compiled-in webpki-roots. Zero code changes, both problems solved.
- **Decision:** **(B).** `ureq = { version = "2", features = ["native-certs", "json"] }` in the app crate; `ureq = { version = "2", default-features = false }` (types only) added to winmux-core. Works for any content filter whose installer drops a root into the Windows store (Netfree, Etrog, Rimon, …) — no per-vendor logic, no fallback path. Caveat: the store is snapshotted once per process; a root installed while the app runs needs an app restart.

### 2026-07-14 — hook-debug.log legacy-line prompt-injection follow-up (v0.4.5 candidate)
- **Context:** a user's Claude Code session ran `cat ~/.winmux/hook-debug.log` and read what looked like a real `<system-reminder>` block — a prompt-injection payload embedded in the log. Audit trace: all current `hook_dlog` / `hook_vlog` call sites in `app/src-tauri/cli/src/main.rs` (12 sites, `:132`–`:2151`) pass **enum-like metadata only** (`tool_name`, `permission_mode`, `session_id`, `pane_id`, `request_id`, `payload_keys` — keys not values, plus RPC error strings). Rule #1 verdict for the current write side: **compliant**. The tainted line the user saw was written by a pre-v0.4.4 CLI, where the static-fallback branch embedded the policy `reason` (which quotes verbatim `tool_input.command` text — see `94ba208` and the v0.4.4-beta.1 entry below). Log rotation is size-only (`LogJanitor` at 1 MiB in `app/src-tauri/server/internal/config/config.go:82-117`), so on quiet installs the legacy lines survive indefinitely.
- **Options considered:** (A) internal binary log format — overkill, kills grep-ability; (B) `sanitize_log_field()` at write time — cheap, catches future regressions of the same shape; (C) `[HOOK-META]` line-prefix wrap — cosmetic, weak; (D) warning header in the log file — cheap belt-and-braces; **(E) one-time rotate on CLI startup** — closes the historical gap for every user, no user action required.
- **Status / Decision:** shipped **(E)** now, deferred (B) + (D) to a follow-up if the audit surfaces a real second write path. Yossi approved after the audit walkthrough.
- **Outcome / Commit:** `hook_dlog` now runs `migrate_hook_log_if_legacy` behind a `std::sync::Once` (`app/src-tauri/cli/src/main.rs` — new function + `HOOK_LOG_MIGRATION` static; docstring on `hook_dlog` updated to spell out Rule #1 for future editors). Marker file `~/.winmux/.hook-log-migrated-v044` gates the check; existing `hook-debug.log` is renamed to `hook-debug.log.legacy-<unix_ts>` (preserved, not deleted, so operators can inspect). Best-effort — every I/O call swallows errors so the hook path never aborts because of the migration. Version bump + release notes still owed (v0.4.5 point-release; not emergency). Immediate advice for affected users: `rm ~/.winmux/hook-debug.log` — the migration handles the rest on the next hook fire.
- **Follow-ups worth watching:**
  - `derive_hook_title` / `derive_hook_summary` (`main.rs:1131-1177`) build strings from raw payload fields and today feed the `feed.push` RPC / desktop card path — **not** `hook_dlog`. Any refactor that pipes `title` / `summary` through `hook_dlog` would reintroduce the same class of bug.
  - `addon_logs` Tauri command (`app/src-tauri/src/addons.rs:889-903`) tails the log into the desktop UI's "Addon Logs" panel — a downstream LLM reading that panel is a symmetric injection sink; kept in mind for the sanitize / wrap follow-up.
- **Update (2026-07-15):** **v0.4.5-beta.1 released** (manifest bumped, `ba35a54`) — the owed release is out and this thread is closed. The deferred hardening (B + D) stays conditional on a second write path ever surfacing.

Second public beta on 0.4.4 (same version; the tag suffix distinguishes it). Cut directly off `main`, which now carries the beta.1 UX wave plus five new items:

Setting-gated (`notifications.pane_pulse_on_activity`, default on). Ring drawn as an inset overlay so it survives a workspace-colour border and tight pane packing.
- **Workspace groups (cmux-A A2)** (`4fd7689`) — collapsible sidebar sections with a color swatch, count, chevron, context menu, and move-to-group; backend `WorkspaceGroup` type + `Workspace.group_id` + `workspace_group_create/update/delete/set_group`. A2 was cherry-picked onto main and re-iconified to Lucide to match the rest of the chrome.

### 2026-07-06 — v0.4.4-beta.1 — desktop UX wave (cut off `ux-improvements-v0.4.4`)
Public beta of a cohort-feedback UX wave. **8 changes, all on `ux-improvements-v0.4.4`, merged to main for the beta cut:**
- **Auto-connect on secondary panels** (`dfe45b5`) — opening Monitor/Files/Browser/Ports in a disconnected SSH workspace headlessly arms the SSH handle first (idempotent, PTY/tmux-free) with a "מחבר…" pill; no more "no active SSH session".
- **Unified new-connection picker** (`b36e693`) — one modal to pick directory + launch command (claude/--continue/--resume/--skip-permissions/plain/custom) together; existing-tmux attach stays a direct action. Connect-time, no persistence.
- **hook-debug.log trimmed** (`94ba208`) — concise one-liners (REQ/ACK/auto-allow/passive), ~50-65% fewer lines; full trace behind `WINMUX_HOOK_VERBOSE=1`. Rule-#1: dropped the policy `reason` (can embed command text) from the static-fallback log lines.
- **i18n hook feed-cards** (`f399b6e`) — kind/subkind/state codes translated en/he/ar/ru.
- **Hook notifications** (`350f670`) — Stop → "your turn" toast **gated by window focus** (Stop fires per-turn; no toast while winmux is focused) + response_summary; SessionEnd → toast default-ON with elapsed duration; SessionStart + Notification **dropped** (CLI silent-ack, removed from bundled spec, BUNDLED_CLAUDE_VERSION 1.1.0→1.2.0).
- **Feed grouping** (`8ebcf24`) — cards show workspace **name** + session badge, collapsible per-workspace groups + "current workspace only" filter.
- **RTL per-line auto-direction — Approach C** (`521e116`) — each visible row gets an explicit `dir` from `detectDirection()` (any Hebrew/Arabic → RTL incl. mixed; pure-Latin → LTR), replacing xterm's "first strong char wins". Safeguards: 23 unit tests (`node:test`), visible-rows-only + rAF-coalesced + per-row cache, Settings escape-hatch `terminal.auto_direction` (default on, i18n ×4), `docs/RTL-TEST.md`. Also aligned the caret arrow-mirroring to the same rule (candidate fix for the PARKED "RTL caret", verify live).
- **Version bump** to 0.4.4 (`703b105`) — app/cli/mcp/tauri.conf/package.json.
- **Deferred to beta.2/next:** mouse-escape leak fix (design ready — Preventive reset-on-connect + Command-Palette "Reset terminal"). See the Open entry.
- **manifest.json intentionally NOT bumped** — beta ships as a manual GitHub download; stable auto-updater users are not pushed the beta. Mobile APK not attached (release APK not yet built).
- **CLI deploy note:** the hook-log + hook-notification changes are in the remote CLI (`winmux-linux-x64`, rebaked at 0.4.4) — they take effect on a remote only after the CLI is redeployed there.

### 2026-07-06 — v0.4.4 UX wave (branch `ux-improvements-v0.4.4`, NOT merged to main)
A cohort-feedback wave off main. **DONE + committed on the branch:** (1) Task 1 auto-connect on secondary panels (`dfe45b5`); (2) Task 2 unified new-connection picker (`b36e693`); (3) hook-debug.log verbosity cut to concise one-liners + `WINMUX_HOOK_VERBOSE=1` gate + Rule-#1 reason scrub (`94ba208`); (4) i18n for feed-card kind/subkind/state (`f399b6e`); (5) hook notifications — Stop "your turn" toast gated by window-focus, SessionEnd toast+duration default-ON, SessionStart+Notification dropped (CLI silent-ack + spec 1.1.0→1.2.0) (`350f670`); (6) feed cards show workspace name + session badge + per-ws grouping + "current workspace only" filter (`8ebcf24`). DEBUG exe rebuilt + live-tested each round.
- **PENDING Yossi approval before implementing (all v0.4.4):**
  - **RTL per-line direction — Approach C (recommended).** Replace `dir="auto"` per row (xterm.js "first strong char wins") with an explicit per-row `dir` computed from the row text: **any RTL char → `rtl` (pure-Hebrew OR mixed), else `ltr` (pure Latin/digits/punct)** — Yossi's rule (mixed→RTL, pure-Latin→LTR). Pure fn `lineDirection()` in `bidi.ts` (reuses `RTL_RE`) + unit tests; ~5 lines in `terminalInstance.ts` `ensureDirObserver`. DOM-renderer only (already forced in `auto_per_line`), copy-paste unaffected. Rejected: (A) Unicode BiDi controls — corrupts buffer/copy; (B) native xterm dir/bidi — confirmed absent from `ITerminalOptions`.
  - **Mouse-escape leak on bare SSH shell (medium-high).** Symptom: `\e[<0;x;yM` (SGR) appears as literal text. **Root cause:** an app (vim `mouse=a`, fzf, less, htop) enabled mouse tracking (`\e[?1000h`/`?1006h`) and exited uncleanly (Ctrl+C / SSH drop / kill) without the disable seq → xterm.js keeps mouse reporting on → clicks in the bare shell send mouse seqs the shell renders as text. NOT tmux (bundled conf is `mouse off`) and NOT TERM (correctly `xterm-256color`). Note: `term.modes.mouseTrackingMode` exposes the live mode (enables a reactive watchdog if wanted). **Fix (Preventive, recommended):** on pane connect/attach, `term.write` the disable set `\e[?1000l\e[?1002l\e[?1003l\e[?1006l\e[?1015l\e[?9l` (safe — live apps re-assert on redraw) + a Command-Palette "Reset terminal" entry (disable set + `\e[0m`, optional `\ec`). Frontend only.
- **Related PARKED:** RTL caret (2026-06-26, §II) — Approach C may fix it by aligning the cursor-arrow mirroring to the same `lineDirection()`; verify live, leave the mirror alone if it complicates.
- **Resolution (2026-07-15 cleanup):** SHIPPED — the branch was merged to main for the v0.4.4-beta.1 cut; both “PENDING approval” items shipped too: RTL per-line Approach C in beta.1 (`521e116`), mouse-escape preventive reset in beta.2 (`6694249` + `85bf082`). The RTL-caret live check stays tracked in the Open (II) entry.

### 2026-07-05 — v0.4.3 SHIPPED — Phase 77 mobile bridge + design pass + unshipped-fivefer
Cut **v0.4.3** off `77-winmux-server` (merged into main). Contents:
- **Native self-hosted push** (no Firebase/APNs): device holds a long-lived WS
  (`GET /api/v2/push/subscribe`); the server fans notification-worthy events to
  it + queues per-device (24h TTL) for reconnect replay. `docs/PUSH-PROTOCOL.md`.
- **A+B hook routing:** A = phone-initiated, server-run Claude sessions; B = a
  desktop-terminal Claude hook forwarded (CLI → local `/api/v2/hooks/forward`)
  so phones get a push too; the phone resolves via
  `PUT /api/v2/session/{sid}/hook/{req_id}`.
- **Per-device scopes** (default: all) enforced in the huma bearer middleware.
- **PATH fix:** `config.resolveHome()` falls back to `user.Current()` so a
  systemd daemon started with `$HOME` unset still finds `~/.local/bin/claude`
  (NOT a regression of 96a879a — the dir was only added via `os.UserHomeDir()`).
  winmux-server bumped 2.0.0 → **2.1.2** so the add-on redeploys.
- **Desktop (unshipped-fivefer):** Notification Center, tray + badge, pop-out
  terminal (true detach + Ctrl-wheel zoom), unified side-panel lifecycle
  (drawer/float/fullscreen), browser-session persistence, FM cut/copy/paste.
- **E2E verified** on a Samsung SM-S938B: pair → session → foreground hook →
  **background push with Allow/Deny from the notification → PUT hook 200**.

### 2026-07-01 — Phase 77 — `winmux-server` first-class refactor — APPROVED, S1 in progress
Yossi approved the design and entered Sprint 1 directly (doc updated in-flight): `winmux-insights` → **`winmux-server` 2.0.0**, clean `internal/*` module boundaries, `/api/v2/*`, OpenAPI+AsyncAPI → Kotlin/TS SDKs, per-client scoping, zero-downtime migration. **Monorepo.** Design: [`docs/PHASE-77-DESIGN.md`](PHASE-77-DESIGN.md).
- **Answers locked:** Q0 Phase 77 (74 taken by split-QR `2ebb645`); Q1 `app/src-tauri/server/`; Q3 workspace = active sessions + subscribers-per-session + pending requests (8a multi-client attach, 8b notification broadcast); Q4 huma recommended, deferred to S4 (S1 stays stdlib net/http); Q5 `workspace_id` server-authoritative UUID.
- **Streaming-contract note (mobile session):** OpenAPI doesn't cover WS frames — the real risk. Pinned §4.4 frame contract (typed frames keyed on `type`, AsyncAPI + `frames.schema.json`, `seq` gap-detection, `frame_version` negotiation) in S1.
- **Cycle break (locked):** `internal/hooks` = thin TCP listener → `core.HookConnHandler`; `internal/chat` owns the hook protocol (`HandleHookConn`, inseparable from session state). `files/logs/workspace` = compile-only stubs in S1.
- **v0.4.2 shipped first** (72-docker-group merged to main, tagged, released) — Phase 77 on fresh `77-winmux-server` branch off that main. Mobile doc `PHASE-77-MOBILE-API-EXPECTATIONS.md` not yet in repo.
- **Sprint 3 COMPLETE (not pushed).** Workspace shared state (`internal/workspace`, the heart of Phase 77): Workspace→Session→append-only Event log (monotonic per-session `seq`, SQLite `workspace.db`, `SetMaxOpenConns(1)`) + live subscribers + pending requests. **8a** = `Subscribe(cursor)` replay-then-stream with seq-dedup, `Publish` fan-out to all subscribers. **8b** = hook_request broadcast → first `hook_decision` wins (atomic `UPDATE … WHERE resolved_by=''`) → hook_resolved broadcast. REST `/api/v2/workspace/*` + WS `…/subscribe` (bearer via header or `?token=`, `hello` frame_version, client→server user_input/hook_decision/interrupt/unsubscribe). `core.NotificationSender`+`NoopSender` (FCM deferred, §7). Tests incl. 10-goroutine winner-takes-all (exactly 1) + a real 2-client gorilla-WS round-trip (both receive; A decides, B sees resolved) + cursor replay — all green. **S3.d — scope refined (Yossi 2026-07-02): no mobile devices in production**, so the legacy Claude-chat HTTP surface (`/api/claude/*`, `/ws/claude/*`) is **RETIRED → 410 Gone** (clean break, `workspace_id` server-authoritative from day one). **Pairing + Insights keep backward compat** (desktop-facing). Chat engine (SessionManager + hook bridge) kept as internal machinery; wiring Claude-spawn into a workspace session is the follow-up when mobile consumes the new API (design §16).
- **Sprint 2 COMPLETE (not pushed).** Files API (`internal/files`, `core.FilesProvider` + sandboxed `LocalFiles`: `..` collapse + symlink-escape reject, list/read/upload/download/delete, size caps) at `/api/v2/files/*`; Logs API (`internal/logs`: per-client tree `logs/clients/{id}` 0700 + "server" pseudo-client, strict name validation, list/read/SSE-stream, 7-day janitor) at `/api/v2/logs/*`; `api.Deps` struct; **OpenAPI 3.1 + AsyncAPI stub** served at `/api/{openapi,asyncapi}.json` (CORS + cache). **Framework flag:** S2 stayed stdlib + hand-authored spec (safety-critical control + one HTTP idiom); full **huma auto-gen deferred to S4** per design §6 — asked Yossi if he wants it pulled into S2. New tests: traversal/symlink-escape, roundtrips, size limits, HTTP, spec-serving — all green + backward-compat integration still passes. Embedded binaries rebuilt.
- **Sprint 1 COMPLETE (not pushed — awaiting Yossi review).** New module `app/src-tauri/server/` (winmux-server **2.0.0**): `core` (interfaces) · `config` (token/janitor) · `auth` · `insights` (metrics, moved) · `chat` (moved) · `hooks` (thin listener) · `api` · `files/logs/workspace` stubs · `cmd/winmux-server`. The Phase-69 cycle is broken via `core.HookConnHandler` (verified: `go list -deps` has no cycle). API serves every legacy route AND `/api/v2/*`. **Backward compat:** installer uploads `winmux-server` + symlinks `winmux-insights`; data dir stays `~/.winmux/insights` so tokens + paired devices survive; detect/uninstall handle both names; `INSIGHTS_VERSION=2.0.0`. Tests: insights/chat/config/api + an integration test (legacy + v2 `/current` round-trip, token enforced) — all green; desktop compiles with the new embedded binaries. **Deferred:** data-dir rename → S5; delete old `insights/` source + `winmux-insights-linux-*` after review. ### 2026-07-01 — Phase 76 — Monitor "Cleanup" tab: reap duplicate port-watchers + flag orphan sessions (daemon 1.2.6)
The `port-watch reap-on-spawn` fix (previous commit) only cleans a workspace when it next reconnects; Yossi still had 4 duplicate watchers for a workspace that hadn't reconnected in 3 days (296h+308h CPU), plus a 9-day orphan `claude` fork session. He asked for the Monitor to detect + kill these.
- **Daemon (1.2.5→1.2.6):** `hygiene.go` walks the process table (gopsutil) → `GET /hygiene` returns port-watchers (grouped, all-but-newest-per-workspace flagged `duplicate`) + orphan claude sessions (heuristic: `tty=="" && has --session-id/--resume && etime>24h` — flagged, never auto-killed, per Yossi). `POST /hygiene/kill {pids}` SIGTERMs via gopsutil, but only PIDs the daemon itself currently classifies as killable (safety — can't kill an arbitrary PID). `markDuplicates`/`argAfter` unit-tested.
- **Desktop:** `insights_hygiene_kill` command (POST, pid-list validated). New Monitor 🧹 **Cleanup** tab (`HygienePanel.tsx`): lists watchers (dups highlighted) with "Kill duplicates", and orphan sessions with a per-session Kill + a "review first" hint. i18n en/he/ar/ru + CSS.
- Together with the reap-on-spawn fix this closes the port-watch CPU leak from both ends (prevention + on-demand cleanup). cargo + go tests green; embedded x64+arm64 rebuilt @ 1.2.6. On `72-docker-group`, not pushed.
- **Resolution (2026-07-15 cleanup):** shipped — `72-docker-group` merged to main (v0.4.2 line).

### 2026-07-01 — Phase 75 — debug.log hygiene (size cap + age retention + Clear button)
The client `debug.log` had NO rotation — it grew unbounded (the v0.3.1 pipe-leak made ~936k lines). Yossi asked for convenient logs that auto-delete after X days so they can't pile up.
- `winmux_core::dlog` now rotates `debug.log` → `debug.log.1` once it passes **5 MB** (bounded to ~10 MB total). `prune_logs(retention_days)` runs at `setup()`: deletes a stale `debug.log.1` and truncates a `debug.log` untouched for > retention (app idle a while). `clear_debug_log()` for the manual button. `0` = keep forever.
- New `Settings.logs.retention_days` (default **7**, serde-default so old settings load clean). Settings → Logs gains a retention number field + **Clear logs now** button (`clear_debug_log_cmd`). TS `LogsSettings` added.
- **Server-side (Phase 75.1, daemon 1.2.5):** my first pass overstated this — server logs were NOT bounded. `insights.log` rotated only at boot (persistent daemon → unbounded), `hook-debug.log` appended per tool-call with no rotation (the same shape as the v0.3.1 936k-line blowup), `mobile-install.log` appended per install. Fixed with a `logJanitor` goroutine in the daemon (the one long-running server process): at boot + every 30 min it `rotateCopyTruncate`s insights.log / hook-debug.log (1 MB) and mobile-install.log (512 KB) — copy-truncate so an open O_APPEND writer isn't broken — and age-prunes any winmux log stale > 7 days. 2 daemon tests. Server retention is a fixed 7 days (the daemon doesn't see the desktop's setting); noted for a future flag.
- cargo check + tsc green. Debug exe refreshed. On `72-docker-group`, not pushed.
- **Resolution (2026-07-15 cleanup):** shipped — `72-docker-group` merged to main (v0.4.2 line). The server-side retention-flag follow-up is tracked in Open (“Small deferred follow-ups”).

### 2026-07-01 — Phase 73 — unified subsystem-tagged logging + mobile-install black box fixed
Yossi hit `✗ nginx install did not confirm success: (no output)` and asked for one organized log place with per-service tags (best practice: `MONITOR` / `INSTALL` / `TUNNEL` …) instead of scattered, swallowed output.
- **Root cause of "(no output)":** the nginx install script ran every step with `>/dev/null 2>&1` and `set -e`, so on any failure (or the 180s timeout during a first-time `apt install` of certbot + the DNS plugin) it exited with an empty channel — a total black box.
- **Fix:** the script now `tee`s its whole transcript to a **unified server-side log** `~/.winmux/logs/mobile-install.log`, emits clear `[mobile] …` step markers + a `[mobile] FAILED: <reason>` line, chowns the log back to the SSH user, and no longer swallows stderr. `nginx_proxy_install` passes `$HOME`, bumps the timeout 180→**300s**, dlogs the tail (`[MOBILE]`, token never echoed → Rule #1/#8 safe), and returns the real reason + the server log path. The nginx-proxy add-on's **Logs** button (`addon_logs`) now tails that file too.
- **Logging convention:** new `winmux_core::dlog_tag(subsystem, msg)` → `[ts] [SUBSYS] msg`. Adopted for the subsystems in play: `[MOBILE]` (install), `[ADDON]` (install/uninstall/detect lifecycle), `[MONITOR]` (insights_fetch). Bare `dlog` elsewhere still works; **follow-up sweep** to tag `[SSH]`, `[BOOTSTRAP]`, `[TUNNEL]`, `[RPC]` across the codebase is deferred (large, mechanical).
- cargo check green. Debug exe refreshed. On `72-docker-group`, not pushed.
- **Resolution (2026-07-15 cleanup):** shipped — `72-docker-group` merged to main (v0.4.2 line). The `dlog_tag` sweep follow-up is tracked in Open (“Small deferred follow-ups”).

### 2026-07-01 — Phase 72.3 — Monitor "daemon unreachable" root cause = `/current` blocked (daemon 1.2.4)
Yossi's debug.log gave the smoking gun: `insights_fetch: path=/logs http=200` **but** `path=/current http=000` — same daemon, same port/token, repeated. `http=000` under `curl --max-time 6` means the request never completed → the endpoint took **>6s**, not auth/liveness (`/logs` answered instantly). **Root cause:** `handleCurrent` ran `sm.Sample(true)` **synchronously in the request goroutine**, and a live sample can block — sequential per-container `dockerStats` (4s timeout × running containers), a `topProcesses` walk, or a hung `disk.Usage` mount — past the desktop's HTTP deadline. The Monitor read that timeout as "daemon unreachable" even though the daemon was healthy.
- **Fix (server-side, daemon 1.2.3→1.2.4):** (1) `Sampler` now caches the freshest snapshot (`last atomic.Pointer[Snapshot]`); the background ticker publishes it (ticker switched to `Sample(true)` so the cache carries top-processes). `handleCurrent` serves `sm.Current()` → **never blocks** on live collection; a slow/hung collector only staleness the cache, the endpoint still returns instantly. (2) `dockerList` fetches per-container stats **concurrently** (worst case = one stat's 4s timeout, was N×4s). (3) Timing self-diagnostic: a sample >2s logs `sample slow: total=.. cpu+mem+disk=.. docker=.. top=..` to insights.log (served by `/logs`) so the culprit phase is visible. Metadata only (Rule #1 safe).
- Also confirms two non-bugs from the same log: the daemon is **persistent** (only one `starting` line at 10:32 across many reconnects — bootstrap re-runs but the daemon isn't restarted, expected); and the v0.3.1 pipe-leak fix holds (handlers show clean START+END).
- Tests: 2 new sampler tests (cache publish + reuse); go vet + full daemon suite green; cargo check green. Rebuilt embedded daemon x64+arm64 @ 1.2.4, INSIGHTS_VERSION lockstep. winmux-DEBUG-test.exe refreshed (11:50). **On `72-docker-group`, NOT pushed** — awaiting Yossi's re-test (reinstall Insights → daemon 1.2.4 → Monitor should show live metrics + Docker).
- **Resolution (2026-07-15 cleanup):** shipped — `72-docker-group` merged to main (v0.4.2 line).

### 2026-06-30 — v0.3.1 SHIPPED — critical pipe-instance-leak hotfix
v0.3.0 regression: sessions disconnected and couldn't reconnect after ~254 Claude tool calls. **Root cause (from Yossi's debug.log — 628k/936k lines were the same `ERROR_PIPE_BUSY (231)`):** the named-pipe RPC handler never returned (611 handler STARTs, 0 ENDs). The pipe RPC is one-request-per-connection, but `handle_client` looped waiting for a next request, and the tunnel bridge's `copy_bidirectional` never propagated the remote CLI's channel-close to the local pipe (russh ChannelStream doesn't surface EOF). So every hook leaked one of the 254 `max_instances`; Phase 66's auto-installed hooks fire on every tool call → exhaustion within a session → 231 wedged everything in a 500ms retry loop. **Fix (both sides):** `rpc_server::handle_client` serves ONE request then returns (frees the instance); `winmux-tunnel` replaced `copy_bidirectional` with `bridge_copy` (half-close on first-EOF). Plus silenced `hooks-check` spam for placeholder manifest hosts (winmux.example.com). Tests: 500 sequential one-shot clients survive (leak would exhaust at 254); 2 bridge-release tests. Cut from the v0.3.0 **tag** (clean, no 69/70 code); merged to main, tagged v0.3.1, released. **My initial bootstrap-timeout hypothesis was WRONG** — reading the real log (not guessing) found the actual leak. **Next: resume the 70 + docker-fix integration** (was paused for this blocker).

### 2026-06-29 — (LL) Claude fullscreen copy (OSC 52) — FIXED (branch `v0.2.x-integration`)
Yossi: Claude's new fullscreen renderer can't copy (neither plain nor in tmux). Root cause confirmed by inspection: winmux loaded only the fit + webgl xterm addons — **no clipboard addon**, so xterm.js silently ignored the **OSC 52** escape sequence the fullscreen renderer uses to copy its selection (alt-screen + SGR-mouse steals drag-selection, so OSC 52 is the path). Fix: `npm i @xterm/addon-clipboard` (0.2.0, matches xterm 6.0.0) and `loadAddon(new ClipboardAddon(undefined, provider))` in `terminalInstance.ts`. The provider is **WRITE-ONLY**: `writeText` → `navigator.clipboard.writeText` (already proven to work — copy-on-select uses it, so no new capability needed), and `readText` returns `""` so a remote program can NEVER answer-back an OSC 52 read query and exfiltrate the local clipboard. Logs `[osc52] copied N chars` (length only, Rule #1). tsc green. Built into the combined exe.

### 2026-06-26 — (HH) RTL arrow-key mirroring — DONE (branch `66-67-extras`)
On a Hebrew/Arabic terminal line the visual Left/Right is the opposite logical direction, so the arrows feel inverted. Implemented in `terminalInstance.ts` `attach()` `onData`: when the new setting `terminal.mirror_arrows_rtl` (default true) is on AND the cursor's line is predominantly RTL, swap the horizontal cursor-key sequence — `\e[C`↔`\e[D` and the application-cursor-mode `\eOC`↔`\eOD` (so it's correct in both shell line-editing and TUIs). RTL detection = per Yossi's choice: the live buffer line at `baseY+cursorY`, strong-RTL vs Latin code-point count (`isRtlText`). Only RTL lines are affected → LTR usage untouched, safe to default on. Setting plumbed: `settings.rs` TerminalSettings + ts-rs binding + `settings.ts` + SettingsModal (Terminal → RTL section toggle) + i18n en/he/ar/ru; applied at load + on `settings:changed` via `setMirrorArrowsRtl`. Built into the current `app.exe`. Next: GG (MD viewer), then II (RTL caret).

### 2026-06-26 — (JJ) port-watcher leak — FIXED
Every connect spawns a remote `winmux port-watch` tokio task (per workspace). The only cleanup, `clear_workspace_detection`, was called solely when the user manually toggled auto-port-forward OFF — never on disconnect/shutdown. The watcher task self-cleaned only on channel Eof/Close, which is unreliable when the SSH transport drops, so dead tasks lingered and accumulated over a long session. Fixes (main, `lib.rs`):
1. **Abort on disconnect:** the session-end task now aborts the workspace's watcher (task `.abort()` + remove from `port_watchers`/`port_watcher_tasks`) in the existing `!still_alive` "last SSH session gone" branch — right next to the forward teardown. dlog: `port-watch[ws]: workspace disconnected, watcher stopped`.
2. **Self-healing dedup:** `spawn_port_watcher` now skips only if the existing task is genuinely alive (`!is_finished()`); a stale/finished slot is aborted + replaced with a fresh watcher on the current session (locks taken singly, never nested, to match `clear_workspace_detection`'s order → no deadlock). Caps live watchers to ≤1/workspace and prevents a stale entry from permanently blocking respawn.
**Reverse-tunnel checked (Yossi #4):** no equivalent leak — forwarded channels are handled by the `SshClient` russh callback (dies with the session); `bridge_to_pipe` tasks are per-incoming-connection and end when the connection closes. The internal-ports set is already cleaned in the same session-end path. Shutdown abort-all skipped intentionally (process exit reclaims all tokio tasks; the during-session leak is what mattered). Built into the current `app.exe`.

### 2026-06-26 — Phase 65.R wizard: connect-to-existing not reached + root trap — fix + new build
**Yossi's report:** "Connect to existing server" connects straight as root, no user-picker; he wants to attach `runner` (a real non-root user), but the existing-user picker never appears. Confirmed he WAS on the "existing" radio; the pre-R standalone button used to show the flow, the new radio doesn't.
**Investigation (code is otherwise correct end-to-end):** `ProvisioningWizard` routes `mode==="existing"` → `ConnectExistingFlow` (verified), which calls `connect_existing_discover` → user-picker → `connect_existing_execute` → `finalize_workspace` saves `user: new_user` + key (NOT root/password) — all correct. Two real defects found:
1. **Root trap (backend):** `connect_existing_discover_inner` built the pickable list with `real = uid==0 || (uid>=1000 …)` — it **included root**. On a fresh VPS (root-only) `users=["root"]`, and `ConnectExistingFlow` seeded `existingUser=users[0]`(=root) with default mode "existing" → the natural "connect" re-created a ROOT workspace. Fix: `real = uid>=1000 && uid<65000` (root excluded); frontend now forces mode="new" when the list is empty and never seeds the auth user; "existing" radio disabled + hint (`connectExisting.mode.existing.none`) when no non-root accounts.
2. **Mode-switch fragility / discoverability (UX):** the radio gave no strong "active" signal and (per Yossi) the existing radio didn't take. Hardened: whole row carries `onClick` (not just the native radio's change event, which a webview can swallow), added 🆕/🔗 icons, an `.active` highlight, and a `console.log("[provision] mode → …")` on each switch so a "stuck in new mode" report is diagnosable via `winmux dev console-tail`.
**Built** desktop `target\debug\app.exe` (main). **Action: Yossi retests — pick 🔗 "existing", confirm `runner` appears in the picker; if it still shows the new-mode form, send the console-tail line.** Label-text polish (clearer mode wording per Yossi) + auto-detect-winmux-on-server deferred to a follow-up UX round.
**Process note:** Phase 65.A/B/C connect-existing was never actually exercised end-to-end before now (this UX trap blocked it). Smoke-test of the new build is required before the v0.2.9 cut.
**Follow-up (confirmed root cause):** Yossi clarified — it was an EXISTING server with a `runner` user (already winmux-connected from another machine), and the screen he saw had only the **"התחבר ובדוק"** button (the *new-server* mode's button) with NO user-picker, then it saved a root workspace. So the UI was stuck in **new mode**: clicking the "existing" radio never flipped it, and he ran the new-server flow to completion as root. Bulletproof fix: `mode` now defaults to **`null`** — the connect step shows ONLY the two choice cards (🆕 / 🔗) until the user explicitly picks, so the new-server form can't be filled/submitted by accident. Added a `console.log("[connect-existing] discovered users=… → step=choose")` so a skipped picker is traceable. (Multi-machine join is supported: the backend mints a per-machine key `winmux-runner@host-<machine>-<uuid>` and appends to runner's authorized_keys without clobbering the other machine.)
- **Resolution (2026-07-15 cleanup):** fix shipped in the v0.2.9 line.

### 2026-06-29 — (KK) Notification UX: friendly text + per-event toggles — DONE (branch `66-hooks`)
Yossi's screenshot: a Windows toast dumped the **raw hook payload JSON** (`{"cwd":…,"hook_event_name":"SessionStart",…}`), and SessionStart toasted at all (noise). Cause: `feed.push` called `show_toast(&title,&summary)` unconditionally, and the CLI-derived `summary` for lifecycle events was the JSON blob. Fix (desktop-side, `rpc_server.rs` — no CLI rebuild):
- **`humanize_notification(subkind, payload, lang)`** → friendly (title, body): session-start "Session started in {cwd}", session-end "Session ended — {cwd}", stop "Finished task in {cwd}", notification → the payload `message`, pre-tool-use(gate) → title "Claude wants to run: {tool}" + body = command clipped to 100 chars. English + Hebrew first-class (ar/ru → en for the toast text; the Settings labels are fully translated). The feed CARD still uses the original title/summary; only the TOAST is humanized.
- **Per-event toggles** in `settings.notifications` (master `toast_enabled` + `toast_session_start`=OFF, `toast_session_end`=OFF, `toast_stop`=ON, `toast_notification`=ON, `toast_gate`=ON, `toast_block`=ON). `hook_toast_enabled()` gates the toast; the Block toast is gated by `toast_block`. Older settings.json upgrades to these defaults via serde.
- **Settings → Notifications UI**: a "Toast per Claude event" group with the 6 toggles (disabled when the master is off). i18n en/he/ar/ru. ts-rs `Notifications` binding regenerated. tsc + cargo green. (No `docs/PHASE-66-DESIGN.md` exists; logging here is canonical.)

### 2026-06-26 — Phase 66 CRITICAL wiring fix: env-file loaded AFTER the env-gate (root cause of "winmux unreachable" + blocking) — branch `66-hooks`
Verified the hook output format against the current Claude Code docs (via claude-code-guide): `{"hookSpecificOutput":{"hookEventName":"PreToolUse","permissionDecision":"allow"|"deny"|"ask","permissionDecisionReason"}}` is correct/current, and — importantly — **`permissionDecision:"ask"` overrides `bypassPermissions` by design** (that's why the old "ask"-on-unreachable blocked even in bypass). The real bug: sshd's `AcceptEnv` rejects `set_env` for `WINMUX_*` on a plain (non-tmux) shell, so the hook only gets those vars from `~/.winmux/run/last.env` — but `load_fallback_env_file()` was called lazily *inside* `rpc_call`, i.e. AFTER the env-gate had already read `WINMUX_PANE_ID` (unset) and bailed. Net: a real winmux session looked like "not winmux" → old CLI returned `ask` (blocked everything, even in bypass); new CLI returned `allow` but never actually gated (no cards). **Fix:** call `load_fallback_env_file()` as the FIRST line of the `claude-hook` handler, so the env-gate, permission-mode shortcut, AND the RPC dial all see the real pane id + socket + token → the hook reaches the desktop and the 3-state engine works. Also fixed the lifecycle-event matcher `"*"` → `""` (per docs, `"*"` is a literal, not a wildcard). Rebuilt `winmux-linux-x64` (sha `15e9845e`). **Deploy: a `66-hooks`-inclusive desktop build + reconnect auto-uploads this CLI.**

### 2026-06-26 — Phase 66 round 2 (partial): hooks Settings UI toggles — DONE (branch `66-hooks`)
Exposed the round-1 backend switches in Settings → Hooks: **"Use the 3-state policy engine"** (`hooks.policy_enabled`) and **"Auto-install hooks on connect"** (`hooks.auto_install`), each with a hint, mirroring the existing `enabled` checkbox + i18n en/he/ar/ru. tsc clean. Remaining round-2 work (deferred, bigger): **66.E** swap the `notify-rust` toast for `tauri-plugin-notification` (the current toast silently fails on Windows w/o AppUserModelID), **66.F** a richer policy editor (view/edit the block/gate lists — round 1 uses built-in lists), **66.G** notification-click → focus app + pane. Those need their own round.
- **Resolution (2026-07-15 cleanup):** shipped; the deferred 66.E/66.F/66.G are tracked in Open (“Phase 66 round-2 leftovers”).

### 2026-06-26 — Phase 66.D.x — env-gate fallback: ALLOW when not in a winmux pane
Yossi's screenshot showed Claude Code popping `PreToolUse:Bash requires confirmation: not running in winmux session` — its built-in "Do you want to proceed?" prompt — on EVERY tool call in a non-winmux terminal. Cause: `cli/main.rs` returned `permissionDecision: "ask"` from the env-gate (WINMUX_PANE_ID unset). That noise is exactly why the hooks were hated. Fix: return **`"allow"`** instead — if winmux isn't in the session there's no policy to apply, so get out of the way. Only when WINMUX_PANE_ID is set does the 3-state engine run. Rebuilt `winmux-linux-x64` (sha `9fb805ef…`). Lifecycle hooks already no-op'd silently — unchanged.

### 2026-06-26 — Phase 66 round 1 (branch `66-hooks`, NOT v0.2.9 → v0.2.10/v0.3.0) — built, awaiting smoke-test
Re-enabling the shelved Claude Code Hooks with a **3-state policy engine** instead of "block everything." Root cause of the old breakage (66.A + Yossi): in `default` permission_mode EVERY matched tool went to a blocking card; worse, the hook RPC sometimes never reached the desktop, so the 120s wait timed out → conservative deny → Claude couldn't run a single tool (not even a read). Round 1 (built, on branch, **not pushed to remote**):
- **66.C — `winmux-policy` crate** (new, pure-std, zero deps so both the Tauri app and the lean CLI link it). `Decision{Auto,Gate,Block}`, a quote-aware `split_chained_command` (splits `&& || | ; & \n`), built-in BLOCK patterns (rm -rf /, mkfs, dd of=/dev/, drop database, fork bomb, …) and GATE patterns (sudo, rm -rf <path>, curl|sh, git push --force, chmod 777, …). Block beats gate; checked whole-command AND per-segment. 8 unit tests.
- **66.D — desktop engine** in `rpc_server` feed.push: pre-tool-use → `winmux_policy::evaluate` → Auto = instant allow (no card) + passive audit feed item; Block = instant deny + toast + audit item; Gate = the existing blocking card. Gated by new `hooks.policy_enabled` (default true). Only Gate blocks → no more stalls.
- **66.D.1 — CLI static fallback**: same evaluator runs remote-side when the desktop is unreachable. Block→deny, Auto→allow, Gate→allow (nobody to approve → keep Claude moving). Replaces the old "ask"/deny-on-timeout fallback.
- **66.D.2 — reliability**: new `ping` RPC; CLI healthchecks the tunnel before the blocking push (fast static fallback if down), retries the push on *connection* error (200/500ms backoff, never on a timeout verdict), and logs every branch/decision to `~/.winmux/hook-debug.log`.
- **66.B — auto-install**: bootstrap runs `winmux setup-hooks --agent claude --source bundled` on connect (idempotent, no --force, no-op without Claude Code). Gated by new `hooks.auto_install` (default true).
- Built: desktop `target\debug\app.exe` + cross-built `winmux-linux-x64` (sha `be68621c…`) + manifest. **Action: Yossi smoke-tests behavior; if green → Round 2 (66.E notif plugin, 66.F Settings UI, 66.G click-to-focus).** Round 1 ships built-in defaults only (no policy UI yet).
- **Resolution (2026-07-15 cleanup):** smoke-tested and shipped via the v0.2.x-integration → v0.3.0 line. Round-2 leftovers (66.E/F/G) tracked in Open.

### 2026-06-25 — Phase 65 FF round 3 (build-15) — `ae0fbc8` — root cause found
Build-14's emit_exit dlog nailed it: when Claude quits, the debug.log shows `ssh-disconnect: clean Eof` + `pty:exit reason=exit 0` — the **interactive bash itself exits on a stray EOF** right after Claude returns (NOT a network drop, NOT a kill, NOT the removed `exec claude`). Fix: the Smart Connect launcher now chains `cmd; exec "${SHELL:-bash}"` — after Claude returns, bash immediately replaces itself with a fresh interactive shell (never reading the stray EOF), so the PTY/SSH stays alive and the user lands at a prompt. Generalizes Yossi's `claude; exec bash` to `$SHELL`; applies to the Run-command launcher too. (If this still drops, the EOF is persistent in the PTY and the next lever is `IGNOREEOF` in the injected shell setup.)

### 2026-06-25 — Phase 65 EE + FF round 2 (build-14) — `10b5855`
- **(EE) conf reverted — `mouse on` garbled Claude's display.** The round 4-5 conf (`mouse on` + extra binds) made Claude Code's live output spawn "a new line every second." Reverted the conf to the last known-good `1e4ca9a` (mouse off, original WheelUpPane binds, OSC8 hyperlinks; sha `274a97f6`). To keep tmux wheel scrollback, mouse-on is now injected per-session via the new-session chain: `exec tmux -f conf new-session -A -s NAME \; set -g mouse on`. Removed the round-4 `tmux source-file` auto-apply (it would re-source the mouse-off conf and fight the per-session mouse-on). If `mouse on` itself turns out to be the corruption cause (not the binds), next step is to drop the injection and accept manual toggling.
- **(FF) `exec claude` removal (build #13) wasn't enough — still diagnosing.** Claude quit still drops the connection, with OR without tmux. Interactive bash (`request_shell`) should survive a child's exit, and the SessionEnd/Stop hooks are passive (silent ack → feed.push, no disconnect), so the mechanism isn't visible statically. Added dlog at every exit path — `emit_exit` logs the reason, rpc `pane.disconnect` logs remote-triggered disconnects — to pair with the existing `ssh-disconnect` Eof/Close/transport lines. **Action: Yossi repros FF, then sends the debug.log lines around the disconnect** so we see if it's bash Eof / transport drop / remote RPC.

### 2026-06-25 — Phase 65 FF + DD: quitting Claude dropped SSH; disconnect = detach
- **(FF) `exec claude` dropped the connection — `81abf93`.** Yossi: Ctrl+C Ctrl+C to quit Claude also closed the connection, **with or without tmux**. Cause: the Smart Connect launcher injected **`exec claude`** on POSIX (`build_smart_connect_script`); `exec` replaced the shell, so quitting claude left no shell → the PTY process exited → SSH channel dropped. Fix: run the command as a normal child (no `exec`) → returns to the shell prompt on exit, pane/SSH stay alive. PowerShell/Cmd never exec'd (already fine). The tmux outer `exec tmux` is a separate persistence layer and unrelated (FF reproduces without tmux), so left as-is.
- **(DD) auto-disconnect is already DETACH — `001b69a`.** Verified every `kill_session_inner` call site (workspace/pane close, reconnect-replace, app exit) only closes the SSH channel → the remote tmux *client* exits, the *session* survives on the server; reconnect with the deterministic pane name reattaches. Only the explicit "Kill session" button (`pane_kill_session` → `tmux kill-session`) destroys. Added a dlog on the detach path + the connect path (`new-session -A -s '<name>'`) so it's visible in debug.log. So winmux never auto-kills sessions — Yossi's loss was the broken-conf regression (`34e262a`).
- **(EE) terminal wheel "regression" was the conf, not CSS.** `git diff` build#8↔#10 shows terminal code byte-identical; only `.fm-window-body` CSS changed. The wheel stopped because the broken conf made tmux exit (no tmux → no scroll). Fixed by the conf fix (`34e262a`); native xterm wheel handles all cases.

### 2026-06-25 — Phase 65 CRITICAL: invalid tmux key names broke sessions — `34e262a`
- **Report (Yossi):** open tmux sessions "closed one after another"; reconnect showed `exec tmux -f ~/.winmux/tmux.conf new-session -A -s …` → `[exited]` (tmux exited immediately, no session).
- **Root cause:** the round-4 (build #6) Shift+Page scrollback bindings used **`S-PageUp` / `S-PageDown`**, but tmux's key names are **`PPage` / `NPage`**. An unknown key in `bind` is a config ERROR, and `tmux -f <conf> new-session` aborts on a config error → tmux exits before the session starts. Shipped broken in builds #6–#10. Existing detached sessions stayed alive in the server (a new client's bad conf doesn't kill them), but every reconnect failed, so they appeared to vanish.
- **Fix:** `S-PageUp`→`S-PPage`, `S-PageDown`→`S-NPage`; trimmed the meaningless root page-down bind. Conf sha `23e5d936`; bootstrap re-uploads + source-files on next connect (recovers the live server, no kill-server). Added a dlog of the exact `new-session -A -s '<name>'`. Naming was never the bug — it's deterministic (`sanitize_tmux_session_name(pane_id)`, `-A` reattaches); random `new_…` names only come from the picker's explicit "new session".
- **Lesson:** never hand-edit the bundled tmux.conf without validating against real tmux key names — a single bad `bind` aborts the whole conf and takes tmux down. (No tmux on the Windows dev box to lint it; be conservative.)
- **Recovery for Yossi:** old detached sessions are likely still alive — `tmux ls` on the server, or the in-app tmux picker lists them to reattach by name.

### 2026-06-24 — Phase 65 O round 6 (build-8) — `da6c4f7` — RESOLVED (proxy deleted)
**The real diagnosis:** Yossi ran `echo "TMUX=$TMUX"` → empty, `tmux display-message -p '#{mouse}'` → 0. He was in a **plain bash shell, not tmux at all** — yet the proxy still fired and sent Alt+Up, which bash read as history navigation ("scrolling the CLI line"). The proxy was never helping; it was breaking the common case.
- **Fix: deleted the entire custom wheel handler** + all plumbing (`g_winmuxTmuxWheelScroll`/setter, `tmuxScrollProxy` field, the App.tsx pane-persistence sync effect + setter calls; −128 lines). xterm.js's native wheel handling is correct everywhere: plain shell → xterm scrollback; tmux+`mouse on` → SGR mouse → tmux native scroll; tmux+`mouse off` → xterm scrollback.
- Kept tmux.conf `mouse on` (helps native tmux scroll, no harm). One-time console note on mount. **Lesson:** rounds 1-5 chased a fix for a non-problem — should have checked `$TMUX`/`#{mouse}` first. O is closed pending Yossi's confirm in build-8.
- **Build note:** frontend-only change, so touched lib.rs before building to force the `generate_context!` re-embed (the build.rs `rerun-if-changed=../dist` from round 4 is the standing safeguard).

### 2026-06-23 — Phase 65 O round 5 (build-7) — `1724fbe` — THE fix
**Yossi's breakthrough:** with `tmux set -g mouse on` confirmed (`tmux show -g | grep mouse` → on), the wheel STILL didn't scroll and `[winmux O] wheel→proxy` kept firing. Root cause at last: **our proxy competes with xterm.js's native mouse handling.** When an app requests mouse reporting, xterm.js sends native SGR mouse events for the wheel (= the scroll); our capture-phase Alt+arrow proxy preempted that with `preventDefault`, so tmux got arrows, not mouse events.
- **Fix 1 (terminalInstance.ts):** the wheel proxy checks `term.modes.mouseTrackingMode`; if it's not `"none"` (mouse on), it STEPS ASIDE (no preventDefault) → xterm.js sends native SGR mouse events → tmux scrolls. The proxy is now strictly the mouse-OFF fallback.
- **Fix 2 (winmux-tmux.conf):** flipped default `mouse off` → **`mouse on`**. After 6 rounds, mouse-on + native SGR + tmux's `WheelUpPane` bindings is the reliable scroll path, and it composes with the round-4 auto-`source-file` (which would otherwise reset a manual mouse-on to off on every reconnect). **Trade-off (documented in conf):** selection now needs **Shift+drag** (bypasses tmux mouse capture → native xterm selection); Ctrl+Shift+C still copies. This reverses the Phase 60 mouse-off decision — flagged to Yossi; togglable via "Use winmux tmux config" off. Conf sha `fc076e0b`.
- The Alt-arrow/M-Up + Shift+PageUp bindings remain as the mouse-off fallback.

### 2026-06-23 — Phase 65 O round 4 (build-6) — `b3921d6`
Yossi's build-5 test: proxy fires (`wheel→proxy ticks=3`) but no hex line and tmux still doesn't scroll. Two findings + fixes:
- **The conf wasn't loading without `tmux kill-server`.** Reconnecting a pane `tmux new-session -A`s onto the EXISTING session (old conf in the running server). **Fix:** the bootstrap now runs `tmux source-file ~/.winmux/tmux.conf` right after (re)uploading the drifted conf — `bind -n` is server-global, so one source-file applies the new wheel bindings to ALL live sessions immediately, no kill-server. THIS is the most likely reason O never worked.
- **Frontend re-embed reliability.** The hex diag didn't appear → suspected the build embedded stale JS on a pure-frontend change (no `.rs` edit → `generate_context!` doesn't re-expand). NOTE: can't grep-verify (Tauri compresses embedded assets), so the more likely cause is Yossi ran an old exe. Either way: added `cargo:rerun-if-changed=../dist` to build.rs; this round's conf-sha change also forces a re-embed via `include_str!(remote-manifest.json)`. Build procedure rule: on a frontend-only change, touch a `.rs` file before building.
- **Conf hardening:** M-Up at a shell prompt now uses the single `copy-mode -u` (no fragile `;` chain); added **Shift+PageUp/PageDown** keyboard scrollback (real keys, encoding-independent) as a robust fallback. Conf re-hashed → manifest sha `2c3c6a57…` (real change, committed). Wheel still sends `\e\e[A`/`\e\e[B`; hex diag (`sent=[1b 1b 5b 41]`) confirmed in source.
- **Next diagnostic:** if the wheel still fails but Shift+PageUp scrolls, the conf scroll path works and only the wheel's M-Up encoding is wrong → switch the wheel to send PageUp (`\e[5~`) + bind `-n PPage`.

### 2026-06-23 — Phase 65 CC + O round 3 (build-5) — `f895a4b`
- **(CC) window.set_title not allowed.** The dynamic window title (active ws/pane + dirty dot, App.tsx createEffect) called `getCurrentWindow().setTitle()` without the `core:window:allow-set-title` capability → unhandled rejection in the console. It's a legit feature → added the capability to `capabilities/default.json` + wrapped both calls in `.catch(()=>{})` (defence-in-depth so a denied perm never throws).
- **(O) round 3 — ESC-prefix Alt+arrow.** Yossi's F12 diag was decisive: `[winmux O] wheel→proxy … ticks=3` fires and sends, but tmux didn't react — so the handler/flag/capture all work; the **escape encoding** was the problem. The CSI-1;3 form (`\e[1;3A`) needs xterm extended-keys parsing that wasn't active. Switched to the classic ESC-prefix `\e\e[A` / `\e\e[B` (tmux reads leading ESC as Meta → M-Up/M-Down, what the conf binds). Added a hex dump to the diagnostic (`sent=[1b 1b 5b 41]`) so the on-wire bytes are verifiable. If `\e\e[A` still doesn't scroll, next candidates (per Yossi): `\e[5~` (PgUp → copy-mode), or revisit the conf bindings. STILL requires `tmux kill-server` on the server to load the conf.

### 2026-06-23 — Phase 65 BB: FileManager toolbar layout-shift on select — `6eeb406`
- **Report (Yossi):** selecting a file makes the button row "jump," then a double-click opens the folder one row above the intended one. Regressed during 62.A–C.

That ~190px width swing toggled the toolbar's `overflow-x:auto` horizontal scrollbar; with the toolbar's `min-height` (auto height), the scrollbar's height grew the toolbar a few px and pushed the file list down → the double-click's 2nd click hit the neighbouring row.
